    SQL、绑定参数与原始结果
    WITH periods AS (SELECT 'baseline' period, ?::TIMESTAMP a, ?::TIMESTAMP b UNION ALL SELECT 'current', ?::TIMESTAMP, ?::TIMESTAMP)
      SELECT p.period, coalesce(sum(quantity::DECIMAL(18,0) * unit_price),0)::VARCHAR value, count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN quantity > 0 AND NOT starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') THEN invoice_no END)::VARCHAR orders
      FROM periods p LEFT JOIN facts f ON invoice_time >= p.a AND invoice_time < p.b AND (unit_price > 0 AND NOT (starts_with(upper(invoice_no),'C') AND quantity >= 0)) GROUP BY p.period ORDER BY p.period

    SELECT stock_code dimension, coalesce(sum(quantity::DECIMAL(18,0) * unit_price),0)::VARCHAR value, CASE WHEN invoice_time < ?::TIMESTAMP THEN 'baseline' ELSE 'current' END period
